## P35 035 Persisting Messages In Postgres

**原文**：

So far we've been saving our stuff inside a single JSON file. This is a pretty naive approach and it's not at all how real applications handle persistence. I wanted to show you a pattern for how you can take messages, chats and message parts and save them in a normalised database. Let me show you what makes this difficult. In our main.tsf we have a message here with an idea. one, two, three, and a roll of user. And we have a set of message parts here. Now the message is pretty easy to store because it's got just a few static properties. But this array of parts is actually quite difficult to store because we have a bunch of different parts with each different requirements. For instance, reasoning parts have to contain a text part, but tool get weather information. doesn't. You might then be tempted to just store these parts as Jason on your message tape, but that's a bit tricky, and it dodges the idea of having them in a normalised database. Because if they're stored in Jason, then they're actually really hard to migrate and hard to query. So instead, we're going to have a whole separate table just for the parts. In the schema.tsv I've laid out for you a drizzle. schema, which is a way of specifying different postgres tables. For instance, in the messages table, we have an ID for the message, a chat ID that references chats.id, created at and a role. Chats is an extremely simple table, which is literally has an ID, because that's all we need for this explanation. But then part is where we get some real complexity. The way I've chosen to represent part... here is we have some stuff that must not be met. For instance, we have the idea of the part, then we have the message idea that it relates to and then we have the type. The type is going to be what we saw earlier, so text or reasoning or tool parts. Finally, then we have the order of the type and the time it was created at. Then below we have a bunch of optional fields here that refer to the required attributes of each. specific part. So text is required for text, text is required for reasoning, media type is required for file, file name is required for file etc. Now while all of these are optional there is a little trick-up our sleeve we can use, which is we impose constraints against these parts. For instance text text is required if type is text and we use an SQL, expression to make sure that text text is defined if t.type is text. We have a lot of constraints here. For instance, these are the file media type ones. These are the source URL ones. And we even have specific ones for each individual tool that we have. This is great because it means that the constraints that we have on our tools are imposed by the data. And so we have database of level validation for the shape of our parts. The only tricky thing here is then we need to map between our normal frontend UI message parts and our database UI message parts. Inside mapping.ts we have two functions here, map from UI to database and map from database to UI. And both of these are pretty long too, so inside. here we say okay we have our text part and then we say part dot type part dot text text and we even add a non-nullable sign to make sure that TypeScript knows that it's not nullable and we map over all of these here reasoning files source document source URL every single tool and in fact every single tool's data as well also needs this. Insane me. end.ts then I've given you one of these mapping functions and you can experiment here with these different parts to see how they get mapped into the database message parts. And you'll notice how conceptually you end up with a third kind of message. So far we've seen UI messages which are used for displaying in the front end and model messages that are sent to the model, but then we have a third kind which are database messages. And we are really responsible for maintaining those, not only deciding their shape and implementing their constraints, but also mapping between them and our UI messages. So have a look over the code, play with the playground, and see if you can get this into your head, and consider the trade-offs between this highly normalized approach, and just stuffing parts into adjacent column. Let me know your thoughts on this one in the description. score two, that's an interesting conversation to have. Good luck and I'll see you in the next one.

**译文**：

到目前为止，我们一直将所有内容保存在一个 JSON 文件中。这是一种相当初级的做法，与现实中的应用程序处理持久化数据的方式完全不同。我想向你展示一种模式，说明如何将消息、聊天和消息片段保存到规范化的数据库中。让我先向你展示其中的难点。在我们的 main.tsf 文件中，这里有一条包含“想法”的消息，内容是一、二、三，以及一个用户角色。我们还有一组消息片段。消息本身很容易存储，因为它只包含几个静态属性。但这个片段数组的存储其实相当困难，因为我们有大量不同的片段，每个片段都有不同的要求。例如，推理片段必须包含一个文本片段，但获取天气信息的片段则不需要。这时你可能会想把这些片段直接作为 JSON 存储在消息中，但这有点棘手，而且违背了将它们放入规范化数据库的初衷。因为如果以 JSON 格式存储，它们实际上很难迁移和查询。因此，我们将为这些片段单独创建一个表。在 schema.tsv 文件中，我已经为你规划了一个 drizzle 方案。 schema（模式），这是一种用于指定不同 postgres 表的方式。例如，在 messages 表中，我们有消息的 ID、引用 chats.id 的聊天 ID、创建时间以及角色。Chats 是一个极其简单的表，它实际上只有一个 ID，因为对于这个解释来说，我们只需要这个。但接下来才是真正复杂的部分。我选择用来表示 part 的方式是……这里有一些必须满足的条件。例如，我们有 part 的概念，然后有它关联的消息概念，接着是类型。类型就是我们前面见过的内容，即 text、reasoning 或 tool part。最后，我们还有该类型的顺序以及创建时间。然后在下方，我们有一系列可选字段，它们引用了每个特定 part 的必需属性。例如，text 类型需要 text 字段，reasoning 类型也需要 text 字段，file 类型需要 media type 字段，file 类型还需要 file name 字段等等。现在，虽然所有这些字段都是可选的，但我们有一个小窍门，那就是对这些 part 施加约束。 例如，如果类型是 text，则需要提供 text text，并且我们使用一个 SQL 表达式来确保当 t.type 为 text 时，text text 被定义。这里有很多约束条件。例如，这些是文件媒体类型的约束，这些是源 URL 的约束。我们甚至为每个具体的工具都设置了特定的约束。这非常棒，因为这意味着我们对工具施加的约束是由数据决定的。因此，我们在数据库中实现了对部件形状的验证。唯一棘手的是，我们需要在前端 UI 消息部件和数据库 UI 消息部件之间进行映射。在 mapping.ts 中，我们有两个函数：从 UI 映射到数据库，以及从数据库映射到 UI。这两个函数也都相当长。在这里，我们声明：好的，我们有 text 部件，然后写 part.type、part.text text，甚至添加非空符号，以确保 TypeScript 知道它不能为空。我们遍历所有这些 reasoning 文件、source、document、source URL，以及每一个工具——实际上，每个工具的数据也需要这个。真是让人抓狂。 end.ts 然后我已经给了你一个这样的映射函数，你可以在这里尝试不同的部分，看看它们是如何被映射到数据库消息的各个部分的。你会注意到，从概念上讲，最终会出现第三种消息。到目前为止，我们已经见过用于在前端显示的 UI 消息，以及发送给模型的消息，但现在还有第三种——数据库消息。我们真正需要负责维护这些消息，不仅要决定它们的结构并实现其约束条件，还要在它们与我们的 UI 消息之间进行映射。因此，请仔细看看代码，玩玩这个沙盒环境，看看你是否能理解这一点，并思考这种高度规范化的方法，以及直接将各个部分塞入相邻列这两种方式之间的权衡。在描述中告诉我你对这个问题的看法。得分两分，这是一个很有意思的讨论。祝你好运，我们下一节再见。

## P36 036 The Template

**原文**：

One of the most important and consequential things you can do is an AI app developer, is to decide how you're going to prompt the elements that you're using. Prompting elements can be pretty annoying because you end up with blank page syndrome, where you know vaguely what you want to say but you're not quite sure how to structure it. When researching this topic, I came across a really great prompt template from Anthropic, which I have recreated. for you inside main.ts. This template breaks down different elements of things that you can add to your prompt, and most importantly, it structures them in a specific order, because the order really matters here. I've added XML tags around each section here just for clarity, but you may not want to include them in your actual prompt. I've given you a more realistic template below, which includes a- couple of XML tanks but not as many as in the original template. So let's walk through each part step by step to explain what it's doing here. The first part is this task context. This is where you can do some high level roll-based prompting. You will be acting as an AI career coach named Joe. This is like the introduction of your prompt where you just get the LM in the right head space for what comes afterwards. Next up, you can explain to the LM what kind of tone should be used. This is useful in some context, but not in all contexts. Particularly if you want the LM to reply in formal language. The thing about this template is that the most important stuff is structured at the beginning of the prompt and at the end of the prompt. So let's actually go all the way down to the end to see the other important stuff. At the end, we have the main ask, which is the main thing that you're asking the LLM. In this case, we say here is the user's question, how do you respond to the user's question? We also have a couple of thinking instructions, where you can get the LLM to do some chain of thoughts before answering. We'll cover these in a couple of exercises time, and finally have a section on output formatting, which is really important. for constraining what the LLM can return. For instance, here we're telling the LLM to put your response in response response tags, but we might equally constrain it to only return a summary, only written a single sentence. Anything like that belongs in output formatting right at the end of the prompt. What about the things that come before the ask that allow the LLM to complete the task? Well, the first thing I've... the turn context is the background data. This is any retrieve data that you want to put into your prompt to give the LM something to work from. We'll look at this in more detail later. Next up are a set of rules that it should follow. This will often be pretty lengthy and comprise the bulk of your prompt engineering work. If the LM does anything you don't like, you probably need to put it inside. and rule so that it understands the scope of the task. Next up are a set of examples, so examples in puts an output. This is overkill for simple cases, but it's great in cases where you want it to reply in a certain format. If you've heard of few shot prompting before, this is few shot prompting, giving the other lengths and examples of the type of outputs that you want. Next up we have conversation. history, so the conversation so far between the user and the assistant, relatively self-explanatory without this the assistant doesn't know how to complete the question, and it also doesn't know what things it's tried before, so absolutely crucial. So all of this background data is put towards the middle of the prompt, where sure it will influence the output from the LLM, but it won't be as important. as the ask and the first task context. What I recommend you do is go through this template, and imagine how you would apply it to a problem that you've tried to tackle before using a lens, but overall make sure you just read through each section and get an understanding of what it's trying to do. Good luck and I'll see you in the next one.

**译文**：

作为一名 AI 应用开发者，你能做的最重要且影响深远的事情之一，就是决定如何为你所使用的元素编写提示（prompt）。编写提示可能相当烦人，因为你很容易陷入“空白页面综合征”——你大致知道自己想表达什么，却不确定该如何组织语言。在研究这个主题时，我偶然发现了一个来自 Anthropic 的绝佳提示模板，并已在 `main.ts` 中为你复现了它。该模板将你可在提示中添加的不同元素进行了拆解，最重要的是，它按照特定顺序对这些元素进行了结构化安排——因为顺序在这里确实至关重要。我在这里为每个部分添加了 XML 标签以增强清晰度，但你可能并不希望将它们包含在实际的提示中。下面我为你提供了一个更贴近实际的模板，其中包含少量 XML 标签，但远不及原始模板那么多。现在，让我们逐步讲解每一部分的作用。第一部分是任务上下文（task context）。你可以在这里进行一些基于角色的高层提示设计。你将扮演一位名叫 Joe 的 AI 职业教练。 这就像提示词中的引言部分，你只需让语言模型进入适合后续内容的状态即可。接下来，你可以向语言模型说明应采用何种语气。这在某些情境下很有用，但并非所有情境都适用。特别是当你希望语言模型以正式语言作答时。这个模板的特点是，最重要的内容都安排在提示词的开头和结尾。因此，我们直接跳转到末尾，看看其他重要部分。在末尾，我们有主要请求，也就是你向语言模型提出的核心问题。在本例中，我们写道：“以下是用户的问题，你该如何回应用户的问题？”此外，我们还有几条思考指令，可以引导语言模型在作答前进行一些链式思考。我们将在几道练习中讲解这些内容。最后，还有一个关于输出格式的部分，这对于限制语言模型的输出结果非常重要。例如，这里我们要求语言模型将回复放在“response”标签中，但我们同样可以限制它只返回一个摘要，或只写一句话。 任何类似内容都应放在提示末尾的输出格式部分。那么，在提问之前那些帮助大语言模型完成任务的内容又该如何处理呢？首先，我……这里的上下文指的是背景数据。这是指你想要放入提示中的任何检索到的数据，以便为语言模型提供可参考的内容。我们稍后会详细探讨这一点。接下来是一组模型应遵循的规则。这部分通常内容较长，构成了提示工程工作的主要部分。如果语言模型做出了你不希望它做的事情，你可能需要将相关内容放入规则中，以便它理解任务的边界。再下来是一组示例，即输出示例。对于简单情况来说这可能有些过度，但在希望模型以特定格式回复时，示例非常有用。如果你之前听说过“少样本提示”（few shot prompting），这就是它的体现——通过提供其他长度和示例，展示你期望的输出类型。接下来是对话部分。 历史，即用户与助手之间迄今为止的对话，相对自明。没有它，助手就不知道如何完成问题，也不知道之前尝试过哪些方法，因此至关重要。所有这些背景数据都被放置在提示词的中间部分，虽然它们确实会影响大语言模型的输出，但重要性不如“请求”和“首个任务上下文”。我建议你仔细看看这个模板，想象一下你会如何用它来解决你之前尝试过的问题。但总体而言，只需通读每个部分，理解它的意图即可。祝你好运，我们下一节再见。

## P37 037 Basic Prompting

**原文**：

In this exercise, we're going to apply the prompt template to a basic prompting problem. You've probably seen in most chat applications that when you start a new chat, it gets put up in the sidebar with an automatically generated name. For instance, if you ask a question about induction Hobbes, then you might get an output saying induction Hobbes versus Arger Cookers. And that would be the title that then gets put in the left hand side. recreate that with some careful prompting. Currently, our prompt is absolutely rubbish. It simply says generate me a title based on this input. Running this, I get about a dozen different options. When all I want is a single option and for it only to return that option. Your job is to rewrite this prompt using the prompt template. You definitely won't need all... of the sections from the template, but you'll probably need rules, you'll probably need output format, and you'll definitely need the ask and some top-level task context. I advise that you copy and paste it in, and then edit it and try tweaking it until it works. Good luck, and I will see you in the solution.

**译文**：

在本练习中，我们将把提示模板应用到一个基础的提示问题上。你可能在大多数聊天应用中见过这样的场景：当你开始一个新的聊天时，它会自动生成一个名称并显示在侧边栏中。例如，如果你询问关于“induction Hobbes”的问题，系统可能会返回“induction Hobbes versus Arger Cookers”这样的结果，并将其作为标题显示在左侧。现在，我们需要通过精心设计的提示来重现这一效果。目前，我们的提示非常糟糕，它只是简单地写着“根据此输入生成一个标题”。运行这个提示后，我得到了十几个不同的选项，而我真正想要的只是一个单一的选项，并且只返回这一个选项。你的任务是使用提示模板重写这个提示。你当然不需要模板中的所有部分，但你可能需要规则、输出格式，以及明确的“ask”和顶层任务上下文。建议你先将模板复制粘贴进来，然后进行修改和调整，直到它正常工作为止。祝你好运，我们稍后在解决方案中见。

## P39 039 Exemplars

**原文**：

In this exercise, I want to show you the power of exemplars. This is what the AI literature calls examples. Instead of relying on a bunch of different rules, we're actually just going to plug in some desired inputs and outputs. And that means we should be able to simplify our prompt a bit. But we have the same set-up as before. We are generating a title, but we have a couple of examples that I've put together. If we zoom... down I have given you a to-do insight here to add the examples formatted with XML. Once you've done this and you've tested that it's working, you should then go and see how many of these rules that you can then delete. See if you can delete the task context too. You may even be able to delete the output format, maybe even the ask, and it should show you how powerful these examples and exemplars can be. Good luck! and I will see you in the solution.

**译文**：

在本练习中，我想向你展示“示例”（exemplars）的强大之处。在人工智能文献中，这被称为“例子”（examples）。我们不再依赖一大堆不同的规则，而是直接代入一些期望的输入和输出。这意味着我们可以稍微简化一下提示（prompt）。不过，我们的设置和之前一样：我们仍然在生成标题，但这次我准备了一些示例。如果我们向下看……我在这里提供了一个待办事项提示，要求你将这些示例用 XML 格式添加进去。完成这一步并测试确认其正常工作后，接下来你可以尝试删除尽可能多的规则。试着连任务上下文（task context）也删除掉。你甚至可能可以删除输出格式（output format），或许连“ask”部分也能删掉。这将向你展示这些示例和“示例”究竟有多强大。祝你好运！我们稍后在解决方案中见。

好的，我们现在进入解决方案部分，运行后得到的是“induction hub research”。在系统提示中，我添加了一个示例，即一个 XML 标签，里面还有另一个 XML 标签，写着“for each example”。然后我们描述了输入和期望的输出。这里我们设定了规则：标题最多只能有 30 个字符，不过我们还是先试着修改一下，看看是否能得到更好的输出。结果我们得到了稍长一点的输出，其实也还不错。所以这个规则可能还是值得保留的，但我们继续尝试。现在再把任务上下文也去掉，重新运行一次。这次我们仍然得到了非常不错的输出。那我们再进一步尝试：去掉“ask”和“output format”。结果……嗯，开始有点跑偏了。那我们再把“ask”加回来，再试一次。太棒了，我们又回到了之前那种看起来不错的输出状态。由此可见，这些示例的力量是多么强大。我们只用了相对较少的 token，就大大降低了理解负担，清晰地表达了系统所需的输入和输出类型。希望你能意识到，这对提示词工程师来说，确实是一个非常非常有用的技巧。 干得不错，我们下一部再见。

## P41 041 Retrieval

**原文**：

In this exercise, we're going to look at how to handle retrieve data and how to format it in a way that's useful for the LLM. Retrieving data, putting it in the LM and getting it to summarise the answer, is sometimes called rag. Retrieval augmented generation. But the cool kids mostly just call it retrieval nowadays. The setup is pretty simple. We have a bunch of URLs here, and we're going to ask... a question about that URL. We're going to use Tavoli to scrape that particular URL, which as we can see is down here. We grab the raw content from the result of Tavoli Client. Extract and then we're going to pass it into this stream text call. However, all we have in the prompt so far is some task context and the final ask. We don't have some really crucial stuff like we don't have the input. The input being the question that we're asking here. And we also don't have the raw content that we're getting from this scrape result. So your job is to go down to the system prompt and modify it. We want to add the background data and the conversation history. We need some rules to tell the model to use paragraphs and its outputs use QUIT. from the content of the website, and we need an output format telling the model to return only the summary not any other text. Once you've done this you should be able to go up to the top and try the different test cases. We're currently defaulting to just the first test case but you can modify this to one to choose the second and two to choose the third. Once that's done you should see a nice output answering each question. Good luck and I will see you in the solution.

**译文**：

在本练习中，我们将探讨如何检索数据，以及如何以适合大语言模型（LLM）的方式对其进行格式化。检索数据、将其输入语言模型并让模型总结答案的过程，有时被称为 RAG（Retrieval-Augmented Generation，检索增强生成）。不过，如今“酷孩子”通常直接称之为“检索”（retrieval）。整个流程的设置相当简单：我们有一系列 URL，并打算针对这些 URL 提出一个问题。我们将使用 Tavoli 抓取特定 URL 的内容（如下方所示），然后从 Tavoli Client 的抓取结果中提取原始内容，并将其传递给 `stream_text` 调用。然而，目前的提示词中仅包含任务上下文和最终问题，缺少一些关键内容——即我们在此提出的问题本身，以及从抓取结果中获取的原始内容。因此，你的任务是前往系统提示词进行修改。我们需要添加背景数据和对话历史，并制定一些规则，要求模型在输出时使用段落格式，并在结尾添加 `QUIT`。 从网站内容出发，我们需要一种输出格式，指示模型仅返回摘要，不包含任何其他文本。完成这一步后，你就可以前往顶部尝试不同的测试用例。当前我们默认只使用第一个测试用例，但你可以进行修改，选择第二个或第三个。完成后，你应该会看到一个清晰的输出，其中包含对每个问题的解答。祝你好运，我们稍后在解决方案中再见。

---

## P42 042 Retrieval Solution

**原文**：

Alright, let's run this and we should get out the website discusses the changing landscape of software development and it said Gashema, Rouch, Vessel CEO, said Matt is one of the best developer educators in the world. Let's try test case number two here and we'll run it again and see what happens, the website and chooses AI hero and it emphasizes my experiences and members. of the X-State Quarteen. So let's take a look at the prompt to see what it's doing. We can see that it's added in the background data here with some XML tags around the URL and an XML tag around the content. The XML tag around the content is really important because the content inside might contain marked-down formatting. And so having both the start tag and the in-tag for kind of a... formatted content is really useful because the LLM sees okay this is the start of it and this is the end of it and this is the end of the background data section too. Without this the content of the raw content might throw off the LLM. We've got some basic rules here. Use the content of the website to answer the question. This is a nice little refusal rule if the question is not related to the content of the website. I'm sorry, I can only ask questions about the content of the website. Use paragraphs in your output and use quotes from the content. We pass in the conversation history and we add an output format saying return only the summary. So this is how you can handle adding retrieve data into your prompts. If you had multiple websites here, you could even consider wrapping these in a website tag. or even moving the URL inside here instead. I really like XML just because of how expressive it lets you be and of how explicit it makes the boundaries. Nice work and I will see you in the next one.

好的，我们来运行这段代码，应该会输出该网站讨论软件开发不断变化的格局。网站提到，Gashema、Rouch 和 Vessel 的首席执行官表示，Matt 是全球最优秀的开发者教育者之一。现在我们尝试测试用例二，再次运行并观察结果。网站选择了“AI 英雄”，并重点介绍了我的经验和 X-State Quarteen 的成员。让我们来看看提示词，了解它的具体作用。我们可以看到，这里在背景数据中添加了内容，URL 被 XML 标签包裹，内容本身也被 XML 标签包围。这个包裹内容的 XML 标签非常重要，因为内部内容可能包含 Markdown 格式。因此，同时包含起始标签和结束标签对于格式化内容来说非常有用，这样大型语言模型就能识别出：哦，这是内容的开始，这是内容的结束，同时也是背景数据部分的结束。如果没有这些标签，原始内容可能会干扰大型语言模型。这里还有一些基本规则：使用网站内容来回答问题。如果问题与网站内容无关，则使用这条巧妙的拒绝规则。 “我抱歉，我只能就网站内容提问。”请在输出中使用段落，并引用内容中的原文。“我们传入对话历史，并添加一个输出格式，要求仅返回摘要。”因此，这就是你将检索到的数据添加到提示中的方法。“如果你这里有多个网站，甚至可以考虑将它们包裹在网站标签中，或者干脆将 URL 移到这里面。”“我真的很喜欢 XML，因为它能让人自由表达，而且能明确界定边界。”“干得不错，我们下一节再见。”

## P43 043 Chain of Thought

**原文**：

In this exercise we're going to investigate a technique that forces the model to output thinking tokens before it responds. This technique is often called chain of thought, and it can be useful for more complex problems, and for getting a bit more reasoning juice out of a cheaper model. In this exercise we're going back to my route a little bit. I have provided for you here a complex piece. of Tabscript Code, and we're going to try to get the LLM to explain it to us. The real piece of complexity comes in this pattern here, which is called an immediately indexed map type. I've also provided for you an article that I wrote on this pattern, which we can feed into the LLM as background data. In the code we have the contents of ComplexTSCode.ts, and the content of the to article here too. We are already passing these into the system prompt below, where we're using the very cheapest model we can, Gemini Tuba No Flashlight, and we're passing in the code and the article. We have a couple of basic rules here, as well as an ask, asking it to explain the code using the article as a reference, because it's going to generate quite a long output, where we're actually going to output it to out. put.md and so you can see it there. Your job then is to add two parts to this prompt. Your job then is to improve this output. Now in my testing what I found is that the model would just kind of blast through the explanation. It wouldn't think through first what the optimal path to teaching all of those pieces was. And so our plan here is to add an output format telling the model to... return two sections, a thinking block and an answer. Thinking should be wrapped in an XML tag, but the answer should not be wrapped in an answer tag. I'm also going to add some instructions, telling the model to think about its answer first before it responds. We can get quite in-depth with the instructions for how we wanted to think. We wanted to consider the optimal path for the user to understand the code, including... each individual piece of syntax. And my hope here is that you, as a person who may not know TypeScript that well, will be able to understand this mad piece of TypeScript code just from the LLM explaining it to you. Good luck, make sure you refer to the template for how you should format this and where they should go, and I'll see you in the solution.

**译文**：

在本练习中，我们将研究一种迫使模型在回应前输出思考标记的技术。这种技术通常被称为“思维链”，它对于处理更复杂的问题很有用，也能让更便宜的模型发挥出更强的推理能力。在本练习中，我们将回到之前的主题。我在这里为你提供了一个复杂的 Tabscript 代码片段，我们将尝试让 LLM 为我们解释这段代码。真正的难点在于这里提到的模式——“即时索引映射类型”（immediately indexed map type）。此外，我还为你提供了一篇我撰写的关于该模式的文章，我们可以将其作为背景数据输入给 LLM。代码部分包含 `ComplexTSCode.ts` 文件的内容，文章部分也包含在内。我们已经将这些内容传入下方的系统提示中，其中我们使用了最便宜的模型 Gemini Tuba No Flashlight，并传入了代码和文章。这里还有几条基本规则以及一个请求：要求模型参考该文章解释代码。由于模型生成的输出会相当长，我们将把它输出到外部。 put.md，这样你就可以在那里看到它。接下来你的任务是向这个提示中添加两个部分。你的任务是改进这个输出。在我测试时，我发现模型会直接快速地完成解释，而不会先思考教学所有这些内容的最佳路径。因此，我们这里的计划是添加一个输出格式，告诉模型……返回两个部分：一个“思考”块和一个“答案”。思考部分需要用 XML 标签包裹，而答案部分则不需要用答案标签包裹。我还会添加一些说明，告诉模型在回应前先思考它的答案。我们可以非常详细地说明我们希望模型如何思考。我们希望模型考虑用户理解代码的最佳路径，包括……每一个语法片段。我在这里的希望是，即使你作为可能不太了解 TypeScript 的人，也能仅通过 LLM 的讲解来理解这段复杂的 TypeScript 代码。祝你好运，确保参考模板了解应该如何格式化以及这些部分应该放在哪里，我们稍后在解决方案中见。

## P44 044 Chain of Thought Solution

**原文**：

Okay, here's what the output ended up looking like. We've ended up with an enormous thinking block. It creates an entire plan for the article, based on the building blocks of basic types, then it chooses map types and index types, union types. It's really thinking through what the user knows and doesn't know, starting from the most basic concepts and then moving towards the more advanced. ones. It even starts with the more general patterns and then shows the more specific instances. In other words, we're using a really cheap model here and it's acting like a map-pococ, which is not great for me. Then finishes the thinking tank here and then begins the article. It then takes the table of contents that it's written before and turns those into sections. actually creating on the fly code examples to just explain that single concept. Honestly, it's done a really, really good job here, much better than the output.md in the problem file, I think. Let's take a look at our system prompt. We have an output format here, saying return to sections are thinking block in your answer. The thinking block should continue your thought process, the answer should be unwrapped. The answer should be. be in Markdown format using code blocks for the TypeScript code. But the important part, I think, is just above in the thinking instructions. Think about your answer first before you respond, consider the optimal path for the user to understand the code. This is important too, assume the user knows very little about TypeScript. And this is where we get the formatting of the list, which I think is really important for the success here. Create a... list of the pieces of knowledge that the user needs to know in order of dependency. This means that not only are we getting the LLM to output a plan, we're asking it to format that plan in a very specific way, because once it has that list, it can then just write the article based on that list. And outputting it using these thinking XML tags is really great, because we can detect these in the out. and then render those in a different way in our front end. Now, of course, we're not really getting the LM2 think here. It's more like we're splitting the task into two separate tasks, which is the planning of the article and then the writing of the article. And it's pretty nice that we get to do this in a single LM call. So that is the power of Chain of Thought. And it's really, really useful for complicated LM... Lem Tasks. Nice work and I will see you in the next one.

**译文**：

好的，以下是最终的输出结果。我们得到了一个庞大的思考区块。它基于基础类型的构建模块，为文章制定了一个完整的计划，然后选择了映射类型、索引类型和联合类型。它深入思考了用户已知和未知的内容，从最基本的概念开始，逐步过渡到更高级的概念。它甚至从更通用的模式入手，然后展示更具体的实例。换句话说，我们在这里使用了一个非常廉价的模型，它表现得像一个“地图-认知”工具，这对我来说并不理想。然后它结束了这个“思考池”，开始撰写文章。接着，它将之前编写的目录转化为各个章节，并即时创建代码示例来解释每一个概念。说实话，这次的表现真的非常出色，比问题文件中的 output.md 好得多。让我们看看系统提示。这里的输出格式要求：在答案中返回思考区块。思考区块应延续你的思考过程，答案应被展开。答案应该是。 以下是用户需要掌握的知识点列表（按依赖顺序排列）： 1. **TypeScript基础概念**  
   - 类型系统（类型声明、类型推断）  
   - 变量与常量（`let`/`const`）  
   - 函数定义（箭头函数、参数类型）   2. **代码结构**  
   - 模块导入/导出（`import`/`export`）  
   - 类与接口（`class`/`interface`）  
   - 泛型基础（`<T>` 的使用）   3. **工具与命令**  
   - TypeScript编译器（`tsc`）  
   - 包管理（`npm`/`yarn`）  
   - 类型检查配置（`tsconfig.json`）   4. **实践技巧**  
   - 类型断言（`as` 关键字）  
   - 联合类型与交叉类型  
   - 常见库（如 `axios`/`react`） 的类型定义   5. **调试与优化**  
   - 错误信息解读  
   - 类型兼容性排查  
   - 性能优化建议   ```typescript
// 示例：基础类型声明与函数定义
type UserId = string;
interface User {
  id: UserId;
  name: string;
} function createUser(id: UserId, name: string): User {
  return { id, name };
}
``` 这就是“思维链”的力量。对于复杂的语言模型任务来说，它真的非常非常有用。干得漂亮，我们下一节再见。

## P45 045 Evalite Basics

**原文**：

We now understand a little bit about how to implement and prompt AI-powered applications, but we really need to know how to make them better. For folks who are used to writing normal deterministic software applications, you're probably used to writing unit tests, but we can't really write unit tests for the output that we get from an LLM. How do you write a unit test for... saying an article is a good article or not, but one thing we can do is run a bunch of data through our system and check what our system does with these inputs. This approach is called eVALS, and you can think of eVALS as really a playground for making your application better, unlike unit tests that just say pass or fail, eVALS are a great playground for you. human feedbacks of a checking the responses manually, but you can also write scores that give you a score from 0 to 100 on how well the system did on a certain task. There are a ton of options for how you can run e-viles. Brain Trust is a particularly popular platform, as is landfuse, which is particularly attractive because it's completely open source, and you can run it on a docket. container if you want to. Now, if we're going to use a tool called Everlight, which is an Eval's runner based on VTest that you can run entirely locally. It's also one that I happen to build when I was starting out in this space. The place where we're going to be working is inside Eval's Example.eval.ts. We can see that Everlight is being called here with capitals. And we have a small data set here of... input, so what is the capital of France and an expected output of Paris. Below here we then have a task, and the task is what you're going to do. We're going to need to generate some text here and then return the text based on the input, and the input is available here as a string. We then have a scorer down the bottom here with the name of Includes, and what this does is allows you to scor... the output that you get out of 100, or in our case between zero and one. It checks here whether the output includes the thing that we expected. In other words, if the answer to what is the capital of Italy includes the word Rome, then we will return 100%, otherwise we will return 0%. Running the exercise will run Everlight in your CLI, and you'll also... also get a local dev server on localhost 366. You should try to use prompt engineering to get 100% in your scorers. And you can even experiment with adding a couple of extra data points here just for fun. Good luck, and I will see you in the solution.

**译文**：

我们现在对如何实现和提示 AI 驱动的应用程序有了一些了解，但我们真正需要知道的是如何让它们变得更好。对于那些习惯于编写普通确定性软件应用的人来说，你可能已经习惯了编写单元测试，但我们无法真正为从 LLM 获得的输出编写单元测试。你该如何为一个……判断一篇文章是否是好文章这样的任务编写单元测试呢？不过，我们可以做的一件事是将大量数据通过我们的系统，并检查系统如何处理这些输入。这种方法被称为 eVALS，你可以将 eVALS 视为一个用于改进应用程序的“游乐场”。与只会给出“通过”或“失败”结果的单元测试不同，eVALS 是一个非常适合你手动检查响应用户反馈的平台，但你也可以编写评分，给出一个 0 到 100 的分数，表示系统在特定任务上的表现如何。运行 eVALS 的方式有很多。Brain Trust 是一个特别受欢迎的平台，landfuse 也同样如此，它尤其吸引人，因为它完全开源，如果你想的话，还可以在 Docker 容器中运行它。 现在，如果我们打算使用一个名为 Everlight 的工具，它是一个基于 VTest 的 Eval 运行器，可以完全在本地运行。顺便说一句，这也是我刚开始接触这个领域时构建的工具。我们将要操作的位置是在 Eval 的 Example.eval.ts 文件中。我们可以看到，这里以大写字母调用了 Everlight。我们这里有一个小型数据集，包含输入“法国的首都是什么？”以及预期输出“Paris”。接下来是一个任务，这个任务就是你要完成的内容。我们需要在此处生成一些文本，然后根据输入返回该文本，而输入则以字符串的形式提供。在下方，我们有一个名为 Includes 的评分器，它的作用是根据输出中包含预期内容的程度给出 0 到 1 之间的评分（在我们的例子中是 0 到 100）。它会检查输出是否包含了我们预期的内容。换句话说，如果“意大利的首都是什么？”这个问题的答案中包含“Rome”一词，那么评分器将返回 100%，否则返回 0%。运行该练习将在你的 CLI 中启动 Everlight，同时还会在 localhost 366 上启动一个本地开发服务器。 你应该尝试使用提示工程，让你的评分器获得 100% 的分数。你甚至可以为了好玩，在这里添加一两个额外的数据点进行实验。祝你好运，我们解决方案部分见。

现在我们已经在任务中加入了文本生成调用。我们使用的是 Gemini 2.0 Flashlight。我们提供了一个简单的提示，其中包含一个问题和输出格式，然后将问题传入。当我们运行这个时，会注意到几个事项。首先，我们有一个本地开发服务器正在 36 端口运行。如果我们点击这里的链接，可以看到我之前执行的所有运行历史记录……以及一堆结果。我们可以看到，对于“法国的首都是什么”这个问题，输出结果是巴黎；德国是柏林，意大利是罗马。最终我们的得分显示为 100%。在命令行界面中也会重复显示这些信息。这也是一个监听服务器，所以如果我将这里的内容改为米兰（例如），然后再次运行，可以看到它正确地返回了罗马，但由于输入不正确，得分降到了 0%。如果需要，我可以点击某行结果以查看更详细的信息。我能看到追踪耗时多久、何时运行，以及所有预期输入和输出的详细分解情况。随着我们的邮件变得越来越复杂，这一点会变得更加重要。以上就是 Everlight 的基础知识。我们有数据，也就是我们正在通过系统处理的数据点。 我们有任务，它用于测试我们的系统，然后我们有分数，它提供一些背景信息来帮助我们查看输出。干得不错，我们下次再见。

## P47 047 Deterministic Evals

**原文**：

EFALs are really good for driving a bunch of data through your system to see how it responds. But to make this work at scale, you really need to be given some kind of score based on how each test case performed. The simplest way to get started with scoring is with deterministic scores. These are scores which always return the same thing given the same inputs. This makes them essentially free to... run, and they're also pretty easy to write. Here we have a simple system that can answer questions about TypeScript releases. We currently have a very, very bare prompt here just a bit of context and the question. Above here we have a couple of data points where we're asking about the TypeScript 5.8 release and the TypeScript 5.2 release. And then above there we have a set of links here. These are... links to the attack at release notes for that version. Then right at the bottom below the task, we have a set of scores. These scores are going to give us just a bit of signal about the quality of the response. Our first score is going to check whether the output includes a marked-down link. If it does include a marked-down link, then we're going to return one, which will give us 100% in our score. If it does... we're going to return 0. You can use a reg x to check for the existence of markedown links, and we're going to be checking the output here. Then our next score is going to be about output length. We want to check that the result is not too long, and so we'll check if output.length is less than 500 characters. If it is good, then it's short enough. We return 1. If it's not, then we return 0. So I recommend you write the score as first and then go on to actually implement the system. You may need to give it a set of rules so that it always includes markdown links. And that the output is not too long, good luck, and I will see you in the solution.

**译文**：

EFAL 非常适合用来让大量数据流经你的系统，以观察系统的响应方式。但要实现规模化运行，你确实需要某种基于每个测试用例表现情况的评分。开始使用评分最简单的方法是采用确定性评分。这类评分在给定相同输入时，总会返回相同的结果。这使得它们的运行成本几乎为零，而且编写起来也相当简单。这里我们有一个简单的系统，可以回答关于 TypeScript 版本发布的问题。目前我们这里的提示非常非常基础，只包含少量上下文和问题。在上面，我们有两个数据点，分别询问 TypeScript 5.8 版本和 TypeScript 5.2 版本的发布情况。再往上，我们提供了一组链接。这些是……指向相应版本发布说明的链接。然后在任务的最下方，我们提供了一组评分。这些评分将为我们提供关于响应质量的一些信号。我们的第一个评分将检查输出中是否包含 Markdown 格式的链接。 如果它包含一个经过 Markdown 格式化的链接，那么我们将返回 1，这样就能让我们的得分达到 100%。如果没有……我们将返回 0。你可以使用正则表达式来检查是否存在 Markdown 链接，我们将在此处检查结果。接下来，我们的得分将基于输出长度。我们希望确保结果不会过长，因此会检查 `output.length` 是否小于 500 个字符。如果满足条件，说明长度足够短，我们返回 1；否则返回 0。因此，我建议你先写出评分逻辑，然后再去实际实现该系统。你可能需要为它设定一组规则，以确保它始终包含 Markdown 链接，并且输出不会过长。祝你好运，我们稍后在解决方案中再见。

## P48 048 Deterministic Eval Solution

**原文**：

Alright, let's check the results of our e-vals. We can see that tell me about the TypeScript 518 release, add to this output, which includes at least one Markdown link, and is the correct length. That looks pretty good to me. Let's go and check the code. Inside our system prompt, we are now passing in the links rendering them as XML. We are adding a rules section here saying, answer the question extremely succinct. all ways include relevant links, and I've also provided some XML links for marked-down link examples. I found that without these explicit examples it would do really weird things with marked-down links. Then we do the R's down the bottom, answer the question with relevant links reply only with the answer. So that's good, let's now check what R's score is look like. We have the includes marked-down link score at which check. where the markdown links have been found. I'm just gonna sort of trust that this Reg X is good. Knowing Reg X is it, probably isn't, but it seems to work okay for now. We check if the markdown links found.length is greater than zero. If it is, we return one. Then we go down to output length, which is extremely simple. If it's less than 500, we return one, otherwise we return zero. These deterministic e-vars are really, really great. when you have deterministic criteria you are judging your outputs on. In other words, output length, whether it includes a certain type of character, whether it includes references, lists, or sorts. It's great, especially for checking output formats, checking that certain XML tags have been included, and using these you can get a really strong signal on how well your system is performing there. are also free to run, which is unlike other types of devals that we'll look at soon. Nice work and I will see you in the next one.

**译文**：

## P49 049 LLM-as-a-judge Evals

**原文**：

There are situations where deterministic scorers won't give you enough information to be useful. There are things that deterministic scorers simply can't evaluate. When you have that desire, it might be time to reach for an LM as a judge, EVAL. LM's are actually really good at evaluating the output of other LM's, so while you looking at the output of the EVALs will probably all will always be best, and LLIM is a judge can act as an assistant, providing an extra score for you so that you can more easily see bad outputs. We're inside questionouncered.evild.ts, and in our data set we've got a couple of inputs. A question asking what is Chain of Thought Prompting, and asking why do the authors of the paper think that Chain of Thought Prompting produces improvements? We're going to get an LLIM. to grade these answers for us using the paper. The task, which is just down below, as a system prompt saying you're a helpful assistant that can answer questions about the chain of thought prompting paper. We're using the messages array to pass in both a user prompt here and then the actual chain of thought paper itself as a file. I've got the chain of thought paper in here as a PDF. So you won't... need to change anything about the task what you will need to change is down below. We have a deterministic score in here too, just asking if it includes quotes, but then we also have an attribution to chain of thought paper scorer in another file. This scorer uses the create scorer function imported from Everlight above, and I give the new an attribution system prompt to the f- First to do comes in around here. Our first job is similar to what we saw in the Eval file is to pass the chain of thought paper, the question, and the answer given. Our first to do is similar to what we saw in the Eval file, we need to pass the chain of thought paper, the question, and the answer given. In other words, there's a lot of background data going into this. The input will be the question. the output will be the answer given by our system and in this case the expected will actually be undefined so we can remove it. Once those three things have been passed into messages, then we'll define a schema for the response. We're using generate objects here because we actually want to generate two different keys here. The first thing we're going to do is get it to generate a score, so a score. of A, B, C, or D. Each score is described in the system prompt. The answer is backed up by the content of the paper and site sources accurately. B, the answer is somewhat backed up by the content of the paper. C, the answer misconstrues the intention of the paper and D, the answer does not provide sources from the paper. The reason we do that is that asking the LLM to just provide a score out of 10 is... famously a bit of a footgun. LLMs have different biases towards different numbers and so getting it to do A, B, C or D is going to be better because we've carefully described what each one means. Along with this score, we're also going to ask it to get some reasoning for us. We can see here that we're mapping the result that we get from the LLM into an actual score that we can then pass. and the reason behind it's score we can then pass to metadata, which we can use to debug the score if we find it a little bit weird. So what we're going to end up here is a function that really takes in an output from our system and sees how well attributed it is, a little assistant that can just ride next to us and give us a mixture of signal about the outputs. And by the way, where you can view the metadata by clicking on a row here and the metadata will be visible next to the Scora. So good luck and I will see you in the solution.

在某些情况下，确定性评分器无法提供足够有用的信息。有些事情是确定性评分器根本无法评估的。如果你想要衡量一个语言模型（LM）的回答质量如何呢？“好”本身到底意味着什么？你希望回答具有幽默感吗？还是希望它准确或有事实依据？又或者你希望它明确引用来源？诸如此类的问题，你常常会自己思考——Kai，我之前就有一个助手可以逐一检查所有这些指标。当你产生这种需求时，或许就该请一个语言模型来担任评判者了。EVAL 语言模型实际上非常擅长评估其他语言模型的输出。因此，虽然你直接查看输出结果可能永远是最佳选择，但语言模型作为评判者可以充当助手，为你提供额外的评分，让你更容易识别出糟糕的输出。我们现在位于 questionouncered.evild.ts 文件中，数据集里包含了一些输入：一个问题是“什么是链式思维提示法（Chain of Thought Prompting）？”，另一个问题是“论文作者为什么认为链式思维提示法能够带来改进？” 我们将获取一个 LLIM，让它根据这篇论文为我们评分。任务就在下方，作为系统提示，说明你是一个能够帮助回答关于“思维链提示”论文相关问题的助手。我们使用 messages 数组来传入用户提示，以及作为文件上传的“思维链”论文本身。我已经将“思维链”论文以 PDF 格式放在这里了。所以你不需要……对任务做任何修改，你需要修改的部分在下方。这里还有一个确定性评分，只是询问是否包含引号，但此外我们还有另一个文件中的“思维链论文评分器”的归属信息。该评分器使用了从上方 Everlight 导入的 create scorer 函数，并且我给新的评分器添加了一个归属系统提示，大约在第一个待办事项的位置。我们的第一个待办事项与在 Eval 文件中看到的内容类似，需要将“思维链”论文、问题和给出的答案传入。我们的第一个待办事项与在 Eval 文件中看到的内容类似，需要将“思维链”论文、问题和给出的答案传入。换句话说，有很多背景数据需要传入到这个过程中。 输入将是问题。输出将是系统给出的答案，在这种情况下，预期结果实际上为“未定义”，因此我们可以将其移除。一旦这三个要素被传入消息中，我们接下来就会为响应定义一个模式。我们在这里使用生成对象，因为我们实际上希望生成两个不同的键。首先，我们要让它生成一个分数，即 A、B、C 或 D 中的一个。每个分数在系统提示中都有说明：A 表示答案有论文内容和网站来源的准确支持；B 表示答案在一定程度上有论文内容的支持；C 表示答案曲解了论文的主旨；D 表示答案没有引用论文中的来源。我们之所以这样做，是因为让大语言模型直接给出一个 10 分制的分数……众所周知，这有点容易出错。大语言模型对不同数字有不同的偏好倾向，因此让它选择 A、B、C 或 D 会更好，因为我们已经仔细描述了每个选项的含义。除了这个分数之外，我们还会要求它提供一些推理过程。 我们可以看到，这里我们将从 LLM 获取的结果映射为一个实际的分数，然后可以将其传递出去。之所以要传递这个分数，是因为我们可以将其元数据用于调试，如果发现分数有些异常，就可以通过元数据进行分析。最终，我们将得到一个函数，它接收系统输出，并评估其归因程度——就像一个可以伴随我们的小助手，随时为我们提供有关输出的信号。顺便一提，您可以通过点击这里的某一行来查看元数据，元数据将显示在 Scora 旁边。祝您好运，我们稍后在解决方案中再见。

我们先来看看 Scora 的代码。我首先想检查的是模式（schema）部分。这里我们提供了一些反馈，包括一条简短的答案反馈消息，以及一个包含 ABC 或 D 的 z.Enum 类型的分数。这个分数随后会被映射到下方的一个具体分值，因此我们可以确保这里返回的是一个数字。在上面的消息中，我们传入了所有相关内容，包括思维过程文档（chain of thought paper）以及一段说明答案评估内容的文本部分，其中还包含最初提出的问题。我们可以将这些内容用 XML 标签包裹起来，但我发现这并非必要。和往常一样，我们达到了 100% 的准确率，并从输出中获取了大量引文。如果我们点击此处，就能看到“引用评分器”（attribution scorer）的元数据。系统指出答案准确无误，已被文档证实，并包含准确的引文和来源引用。如果我们再点击下方的另一个条目，同样可以看到系统提示“所有主张均有文档支持”。因此，借助 EverLight 和 ASDK，我们得以构建一个能够替我们检查系统输出的助手，这为我们提供了非常有价值的反馈信号，尤其是在出现问题时。 接下来，我们将把这些方法付诸实践，并在裁判评分中对确定性方法与 LMS 方法进行比较。干得不错，我们下一节再见。

## P51 051 Dataset Management

**原文**：

At this point, you might be convinced about eVals and how useful they are, but you might be wondering a little bit how you would actually integrate these into a proper application. And specifically, when do you run them in the dev pipeline? Well, the way eVals generally work at companies is you have three datasets that you draw on. The first is your dev dataset, which contain the eVals that you run with every local change. This is about 5 to 10 E-VALS of your toughest toughest problems that you're working on right now. This is a small set which won't cost you that much to run and will run pretty quickly. The next one is your CI data set. These E-VALS ensure that the merge code commits continue to pass. These will usually run on your CI and could execute with every commit or... could execute with every pull request. It will probably be a bit bigger than your dev data set, but shouldn't be more than like 15 minutes to run, let's say. And the final set is the regression data set. The largest data set containing all the e-vals that you want to track over time. This could be huge could be 500 e-vals. And of course, because you're running your system and probably doing LLM as a judge. as well, it will be expensive to run. You could run these on a schedule if you're making lots of changes or run them on request. I've also added a table below so that you can see them here. Probably the least popular here is CI. Having a decent size, EVAL suite that runs on every commit is pretty committing and so most teams just have a small number of reels. hard problems for death and then a larger suite for regression testing. Now, of course, eVals can move between these different data sets as needed. An eVal might start in the dev suite and then go back into regression once it's been largely solved. Or, if an eVal in the regression suite fails, then it gets moved back to dev for testing. So, this is how you should be thinking about eVals when you're thinking. about integrating them. It's not just one monolithic suite, it is these three groups. With the dev being the most important, the regression being the next most important, and the CI, if you want to. Hopefully that next thing's clearer. Nice work, and I'll see you in the next one.

**译文**：

此时，你可能已经对 eVals 的实用性深信不疑，但或许仍会好奇：究竟该如何将它们真正整合进一个规范的应用程序中？具体来说，应该在开发流程中的什么时机运行它们？通常来说，企业在实际应用中 eVals 的方式是借助三类数据集。第一类是开发数据集（dev dataset），其中包含你在每次本地修改时都会运行的 eVals。这些 eVals 通常是当前正在攻关的 5 到 10 个最具挑战性的问题。由于数量较少，运行成本不高，执行速度也很快。第二类是持续集成数据集（CI data set）。这些 eVals 用于确保合并代码提交后仍能通过测试。它们通常在持续集成环境中运行，可以针对每次代码提交或每个拉取请求执行。这类数据集的规模可能比开发数据集稍大，但预计运行时间不应超过 15 分钟。第三类是回归数据集（regression data set），这是规模最大的一类，包含所有希望长期追踪的 eVals，数量可能多达 500 个。当然，由于你是在运行自己的系统，并且很可能使用大语言模型（LLM）作为评判者…… 此外，运行这些测试的成本也会很高。如果你频繁进行更改，可以按时间表运行这些测试，也可以按需运行。我还在下方添加了一个表格，方便你在此处查看。其中，CI 可能是最不常用的一种。在每个提交后都运行一个规模适中的 EVAL 套件会非常耗费资源，因此大多数团队只会保留少量测试用例，用于处理棘手的难题，然后再准备一个更大的套件用于回归测试。当然，EVAL 可以根据需要在这几个不同的数据集之间移动。某个 EVAL 可能最初在开发套件中运行，待问题基本解决后，再移回回归测试套件。或者，如果回归测试套件中的某个 EVAL 失败，它就会被移回开发套件进行测试。因此，这就是你在考虑集成 EVAL 时应该有的思维方式。它并不是一个单一的庞大套件，而是由这三个组别组成。其中，开发套件最为重要，回归测试套件次之，而 CI 则可根据需要选择是否采用。希望接下来的内容能更清晰一些。做得很好，我们下一节再见。

What changes were made in the latest version of React Query, React Query, recent changes. There is a lot. of data here, I've added 44 different input output pairs. Deterministic score is for length or maybe an LM as a judge for clickability. It's really up to you. I recommend you take some time over this exercise. So good luck. Enjoy your e-valtriving developments and I will see you in the solution.

这是一个有趣的练习，用来展示一种解决方案，因为我真的希望你能思考自己的解决方案以及它有多好。我在实现这个功能时注意到，分数其实并没有真正帮上我太多忙。仅通过目视检查，我就得到了一些自己满意的输出结果，比如“React Query 更新”对比“React Query 近期变更”，“电动汽车研究”对比“电动汽车比较”等等，所以我并没有觉得有必要自己引入……这里的分数。我大部分的工作最终都花在了提示词上：找出最能体现对话核心内容的简洁标题，标题最多不超过30个字符。我还添加了这样一条规则：如果对话中使用了缩写词，就必须在标题中使用这些缩写。这是因为我注意到一个边缘情况——在这个“fang 公司准备 fang 面试”的例子中，如果没有这条规则，系统会生成一些奇怪的结果。但后来……我又添加了这条额外规则，因为对话列表中的所有条目都不会使用“聊天”“对话”或“讨论”这类词汇。这是因为之前系统会生成类似“TypeScript 与 JavaScript 讨论”或“植物问题讨论”这样的标题。 而我想要的是，当用户向下浏览时，内容在视觉上能更有吸引力一些。最后，当然，我还添加了一点输出格式，写上“生成的对话标题”，然后只返回标题。关于 eVals，它们主要是用来将大量数据传入你的系统，看看会发生什么。我们或许可以认为它们是单元测试，但实际上它们更像是一个游乐场，而分数只是为了给你一个反馈信号。但我非常非常想看到你们对这个问题的解决方案。所以现在就去 Discord 上把它们发出来吧。那将会非常棒。非常感谢，我们下一期再见。

## P54 054 How Do I Know My Dataset Is Good_

**原文**：

I wanted to take a minute and critique our title's data set a little bit. The quality of this data set is going to really affect the quality of our e-values and so the quality of our system. But you, I recommend you take the CSV and you import it into Google Sheets or any kind of spreadsheet viewer. Once that's done, you can take a look at the data like I'm doing and assess it. There are three metrics we want to be thinking. about when we're looking at a dataset like this. The first is quantity, which is literally how much data do we have. Here we've got about 44 input output pairs, which is not too bad. The more data that we throw at this, the more weird cases we're going to uncover. Just because our kind of probabilistic and so the more tests you do, the more likely you are. on cover a weird behavior. So data quantity not too bad. What about data quality? In other words, how good is the data that we have? Well, I've manually gone through and checked every individual one of these. I wrote the first 5 or 10 myself so I know they're pretty good and the rest were generated by a powerful LLM. Sometimes you will just have crap data. where the output pair is just like react query lower case or something. And you may have to go through and manually clean it up. But in our case, the data quality is good. The metric I am worried about, though, is data coverage, which is how many different edge cases use cases does our data set cover. What you might notice by visually looking at these is that they're mostly the inputs out. all around the same length. They're each about two lines in this cell. None of these inputs test for malicious intent, either. We don't know how it would react if we ask it to make a bomb or something. They are all in perfect punctuation as well. And they are all asking a very clear question. What happens if the input is just yo? What happens if the input is in a language? other than English. We simply don't know because it's not in our data set. So while we have enough data I would say and the data quality is pretty good, the data coverage is quite poor. So what I recommend you do is take your CSV and add some cases that test for different inputs. Try adding some malicious inputs. Try adding some non-English. queries. You can use an LLM to help you there. Try adding some really long pieces of text or try adding some super short or vague inputs. I've got a list below that you can follow for inspiration. Next try running the Eviles with the new data set to see how it performs. And see if you need to make any adjustments to your prompt to fix it. And of course, let us know how you get on in the discord. good luck and I will see you in the next one.

我想花一点时间来稍微批评一下我们这个标题的数据集。这个数据集的质量将真正影响到我们 e 值的质量，进而影响到我们系统的整体质量。不过我建议你把 CSV 文件导入到 Google Sheets 或任何电子表格查看器中。完成之后，你就可以像我一样查看这些数据并进行评估了。当我们审视这样一个数据集时，有三个指标需要考虑。第一个是数据量，也就是我们到底有多少数据。这里我们大约有 44 个输入输出对，这还算不错。我们投入的数据越多，就越能发现一些奇怪的情况。因为我们的方法是基于概率的，所以测试得越多，就越有可能发现一些异常行为。因此，数据量方面还算可以。那么数据质量又如何呢？换句话说，我们拥有的数据到底有多好？我已经手动检查了每一条数据。前 5 到 10 条是我自己写的，所以我知道它们质量不错，其余的则是由一个强大的 LLM 生成的。有时候你得到的数据可能就是垃圾数据。 其中输出对就像 react query 的小写形式之类的。你可能需要逐一检查并手动清理。但在我们的情况下，数据质量很好。不过，我担心的指标是数据覆盖度，即我们的数据集涵盖了多少不同的边缘用例。通过肉眼观察这些内容，你可能会发现它们大多是输入输出对，长度大致相同。在这个单元格中，每个输入大约占两行。这些输入中也没有任何测试恶意意图的内容。我们不知道如果要求它制造炸弹之类的东西时，它会如何反应。此外，所有输入的标点符号都完全正确，并且都提出了非常清晰的问题。如果输入只是“yo”会怎么样？如果输入是英语以外的语言又会怎样？我们根本不知道，因为这些情况不在我们的数据集中。因此，虽然我认为数据量足够，数据质量也相当不错，但数据覆盖度却相当差。所以我建议你这样做：取出你的 CSV 文件，添加一些用于测试不同输入的用例。尝试添加一些恶意输入，再添加一些非英语查询。你可以使用 LLM 来辅助完成这项工作。 尝试添加一些非常长的文本，或者尝试添加一些超短或模糊的输入。我在下方准备了一份清单，你可以参考以获得灵感。接下来，尝试使用新的数据集运行 Eviles，看看它的表现如何。并看看你是否需要对提示进行调整以修复问题。当然，也请在 Discord 中告诉我们你的进展。祝你好运，我们下一期再见。

Once that's all set up we can get to the interesting stuff which is actually tracing our code. Our first job is to declare the trace variable using the LangFuse.trace method and we're then going to pass it the following arguments which is sessionidbody.id. The sessionid is going to allow us to group related traces together. Since this is the chat ID that is... maintained in the front end so we'll be able to see multiple traces from the same conversation grouped under the same heading. Once that's done we go into the actual logic below here. The way this works is we're generating the text for the title and the string result in parallel. Notice how we're calling generate text but we're not awaiting it. Now the air SDK has some built-in telemetry helpers via experimental telemetry here. You're going to do that for the generate text call and for the string text call down below, making sure you use different function IDs for each one. If we were doing persistence here, instead of just console logging it, we might imagine we might save the chat to the database with the new generated title. But our to do here is to flush the length use traces using lengthfused.flush a sink. This will ensure that the traces get to landfuse. Once that's done you'll be able to go through this loop. 既然我们已经思考了很长时间，什么样的数据集才算优质，并且努力创建自己的测试用例、扩大数据覆盖范围，现在我要告诉大家一个“作弊码”——它能让你自动创建出高质量的数据。这个方法就是：观察用户在生产环境中如何使用你的应用。如果你能持续观察用户如何使用你的应用，并发现他们遇到了某种异常行为，你就可以……将那次交互的数据提取出来，直接用作一个新的评估用例。这种模式非常强大，以至于大多数评估平台也都具备可观测性功能。BrainTrust 称之为“端到端 AI 开发”，而 Langfuse 则提到了“追踪”和“评估”。虽然这两个平台我都推荐，但这次我想带大家尝试的是 Langfuse。我们将使用之前生成的聊天时间数据，对其进行插桩处理，然后将其接入一个真实的应用程序，并通过 Langfuse 对该应用进行观测。我们的第一步是进入 API 文件 `Langfuse.ts`。接下来，我们要从 OpenTelemetry SDK 的 Node 包中引入 Node SDK 类。由于这部分代码属于模板代码，你也可以参考解决方案文件。然后，你需要使用以下代码声明 Langfuse 变量…… Langfuse 包中的 Langfuse 类（或者这些包已经安装好了，所以你应该可以通过自动补全找到它）。接下来，你需要传入一些环境变量。这些环境变量可以通过下面的说明获取。你需要注册 Langfuse Cloud（这是免费的），创建一个新的项目，然后就会获得这些环境变量，之后你可以将它们复制并粘贴到 `.env` 文件中。一切设置完成后，我们就可以开始真正有趣的部分了——对我们的代码进行追踪。我们的第一步是使用 `LangFuse.trace` 方法声明 `trace` 变量，然后传入以下参数：`sessionid` 和 `body.id`。`sessionid` 将允许我们将相关的追踪记录分组。由于这是在前端维护的聊天 ID，因此我们可以看到同一对话中的多个追踪记录被分组在同一个标题下。完成这一步后，我们进入下方的实际逻辑部分。其工作原理是并行生成标题文本和字符串结果。注意，我们调用了 `generate_text` 方法，但并没有使用 `await` 等待它。 现在，Air SDK 通过实验性遥测功能提供了一些内置的遥测辅助工具。你需要传入一个对象，其中包含一个表示函数用途的函数 ID，以及一些元数据，例如长度使用跟踪 ID。你需要为 `generate text` 调用和下方的 `string text` 调用都执行此操作，并确保为每个调用使用不同的函数 ID。最后，在两个 UI 消息屏幕的 `on finish` 内部——这里我们实际上会获取最终标题的结果。如果我们在此处进行持久化操作（而不是仅仅通过控制台输出），我们可能会将聊天内容连同新生成的标题一起保存到数据库中。但当前我们的任务是使用 `lengthfused.flush` 方法刷新长度使用跟踪。这将确保这些跟踪数据能够发送到 Landfuse。完成这一步后，你就可以执行以下循环操作：向系统发送一条消息，在控制台中查看生成的标题，然后访问 Landfuse 云应用查看该跟踪信息。我将在解决方案中向你展示你能从跟踪信息中获取的所有内容。祝你好运，我们稍见。

ASDK 让您可以完全控制流，并将自定义数据部分流式传输到用户界面。这在各种不同用例中都很有用，您可以用它实现一些非常高级的功能。不过，现在我们只做一些相当简单的操作。在使用 LEM 驱动的应用程序时，一个常见的模式是系统会询问您接下来想问什么问题。我们也将把这个功能构建到我们的应用程序中……首先，我希望您查看应用程序中的这个 latest suggestion 变量。我们将把这个 latest suggestion 变量放入这里的聊天输入框中。如果到目前为止还没有任何消息，我们会将其默认设置为“法国的首都是什么？”，否则我们会提供最新的建议。代码上方的注释应该能让我们了解正在发生的事情。我们需要从最后一条消息中获取数据建议部分。那么，什么是数据建议部分呢？它是一种我们将在消息上定义的自定义数据部分，定义方式是在此处声明一个 my message 类型。 我们之前见过 UI 消息用于工具的情况，但现在我们将它用于数据部分。我在下方提供了相关参考资料，说明如何实现这一点。这种类型将是一个建议数据部分，其中建议是一个单独的字符串，并结合参考资料，这应该足以完成操作。接下来我们进入实现的核心部分。在这里，我们执行常规的应用程序逻辑，将一些文本流式传输到前端。我们使用 right adult merge 将该 UI 消息流合并进来，然后使用 consume stream 等待该流完成。如果想知道这段代码的具体作用，我同样准备了一些参考资料。最后，我们进入后续建议部分，这里我们获取历史中的所有消息，然后将它们追加到新的消息中。其中一条将是助手消息，即上述流式文本结果中的文本。换句话说，就是我们刚才通过与模型交互生成的文本。然后，我们在下方提出另一个后续问题：“我应该问什么下一个问题？仅返回问题文本。” 这就是我们的实现方式，你不需要在这里做太多修改，但我们仍然需要将数据建议部分流式传输到前端。接下来，我们将为数据部分创建一个 ID，然后监控文本范围，从这些分块中逐步构建建议内容。每个分块仅包含建议总文本的增量部分，因此我们需要在这里逐步构建完整的建议内容。最后，你需要调用 `write` 方法，将数据部分写入流中。这里有很多陌生的概念，因此我为你提供了大量参考资料，专门介绍如何创建数据部分、理解这些数据部分的作用，甚至如何将它们渲染到前端。所有相关信息都列在下方的文本中。完成这些步骤且文本开始流式传输后，我们将前往 `root.tsx`，最终实现最新的建议内容。我们将获取最后一条消息，从中提取数据建议部分，并将其插入到这里。这应该是你最后要完成的工作，因为该部分会自动被推送到聊天输入框中。 完成之后，你就可以向系统提问了，它会为你推荐一个后续问题，你应该能在网络标签页中看到该问题实时显示出来。祝你好运，我们稍后在解决方案中见。

Okay, we have our dinky little suggestion here, and we can ask, what is the capital of France? We see it streaming in, and then we see a suggestion streamed in. This corresponds to a type of data suggestion and the data being a single string. But at the end, we can see that it's being passed into create UI message stream, which means that the writer.right method all the way below is type-safe. We can see that we get autocomplete if we try data suggestion, beautiful. We are constructing the full suggestion by appending each chunk to it, and then rewriting the same data part to the front. so that it updates in place. In the front end then when we go to client root.tsx we have a little bit of logic for getting the latest suggestion. We grab the final message, we find a data part with the type of data suggestion and then we grab its data property meaning that later suggestion is either string if the data suggestion part exists. or it's undefined if there are no messages or if it couldn't find a data part. And just like that, we're able to stream custom data anytime we like inside a UMS stream and use it on the front end. It's an incredibly nice pattern and it enables so many cool things. ## P61 061 Message Metadata

**原文**：

In this exercise, we're going to explore adding metadata to messages. This is additional information you want to track about the message that doesn't necessarily belong inside parts. For instance, we never want multiple metadata entries per message. It's just one piece of metadata per message. In our case, we want to track how long each message took to generate. And then, of course, show that. the user. This is really useful information especially for long running workflows or agentic loops or things like that. Our first step is to create a my UI message type by passing some metadata to UI message. So far we've used two of the three type arguments before we've used tools we've used data parts and now we're using metadata. We can pass a shape to that metadata to say to a SDK. This is what we want the shape of the metadata to be and then that will be typed safe from the front end to the back end in our case We just want to define a duration number further down. We want to initiate a start time because this will be the start time of the stream And then we have a message metadata function below. This is a property that you pass to a UI message stream. I've added some reference material below on how to implement this. Our next step is to go into the message component and add a metadata prop. You can take some inspiration from my UI message parts here. That metadata should be being used inside here, yet we have metadata.juration, so all you have to figure out is the type of it. Our final to do here is inside the root.tsx file where we just need to pass them. metadata down to the message components. Once that's done you should be able to query your system and get a response back and then see how long that response took. Nice work and I will see you in the solution.

**译文**：

在本练习中，我们将探讨如何向消息添加元数据。元数据是用于跟踪消息的附加信息，通常不需要包含在消息的“部分”（parts）中。例如，我们通常不希望一条消息包含多个元数据条目，而是一条消息只对应一个元数据。在本例中，我们希望跟踪每条消息生成所需的时间，然后将这一信息展示给用户。对于耗时较长的工作流程、智能体循环（agentic loops）等场景，这类信息非常有用。 我们的第一步是通过向 `UI message` 传递元数据来创建一种自定义的 `my UI message` 类型。到目前为止，我们已经使用过该类型三个类型参数中的两个：之前我们使用过 `tools` 和 `data parts`，现在要使用的是 `metadata`。我们可以向元数据传递一个“结构定义”（shape），以告知 SDK 期望的元数据格式。这样就能确保从前端到后端的类型安全。在本例中，我们只需定义一个表示时长的数字（`duration number`）。接下来，我们需要初始化一个开始时间（`start time`），因为它将作为流式传输的开始时间。随后，我们在下方定义了一个 `message metadata` 函数，这是一个需要传递给 `UI message stream` 的属性。 我在下方添加了一些关于如何实现此功能的参考资料。我们的下一步是进入消息组件，添加一个元数据属性（metadata prop）。你可以从我的 UI 消息部件中获取一些灵感。该元数据应该在这里面被使用，但我们目前只有 `metadata.juration`，所以你只需要确定它的类型即可。我们在这里最后的待办事项是在 `root.tsx` 文件中，只需将它们传递给消息组件即可。一旦完成，你就应该能够查询系统并收到响应，然后查看该响应耗时多久。干得漂亮，我们稍后在解决方案中再见。

## P63 063 Error Handling

**原文**：

One incredibly important part of streaming is handling errors. There are many many different things that can go wrong in the A&H decay, and the way it handles errors is really nice, because we are streaming to the front end using a UI message stream. If there's ever an error that happens throughout the course of running that stream, then it will simply stream that error to the front end. We're going to look at how to watch for these errors and customize how to... look in the front end. The first thing you're going to do is go into API chat and I've created a create UI message stream that always errors. We're importing the Retry error from the AI package here because the AI SDK has a load of first class errors that it supports. There really are a ton of different errors here. For instance, when you choose a model ID that doesn't exist for that provider. our case, this occurs when a retry operation fails. By default, the ASTK retries an operation three times and so this will be thrown if the retry operation failed. Our job then is to watch out for this retry error by passing an on-error function to the Create UMS Extreme function. This function is responsible for returning a string to the... front end describing what happened. In other words it allows us to take the error that happened and then stream down a string to the front end. In the front end you can then use this string to display an error message. Our first to do is insert the on error function. We're going to check if the error is a retriera by calling retriera.is instance error. If it is we're just going to return a message that tells the user to try again. Otherwise, we're going to return a default message if the retry is not a retry error. I'm sure you've written a few of these before, and unknown error occurred, or something similar. In the front end, we're then going to grab the error property on the use chat hook. This is either an error or undefined. And below here, just weren't passing in the to do, we're going to render an error message component, which is just hang a- in this file down the bottom here. Once that's done, you should be able to send a message to your system, and whatever happens you should get a retry error, which will show the message that you return from the on-error function inside the error message component. Good luck and I will see you in the solution.

**译文**：

流式传输中一个极其重要的部分是错误处理。在 A&H 衰减过程中，可能会发生许多许多不同的问题，而它的错误处理方式非常出色，因为我们是通过 UI 消息流向前端进行流式传输的。如果在该流式传输运行过程中发生任何错误，它会将该错误直接流式传输到前端。我们将研究如何监控这些错误，并自定义在前端中如何...查看它们。你要做的第一件事是进入 API chat，我已经创建了一个始终会报错的 `create UI message stream`。我们在这里从 AI 包中导入 `Retry` 错误，因为 AI SDK 支持大量第一类错误。这里确实有非常多的不同类型的错误。例如，当你为该提供商选择一个不存在模型 ID 时，就会出现这种情况。在我们的例子中，当重试操作失败时就会发生这种情况。默认情况下，ASTK 会重试操作三次，因此如果重试操作失败，就会抛出此错误。我们的任务就是通过向 `Create UMS Extreme` 函数传递一个 `on-error` 函数来监控这个重试错误。 这个函数的作用是返回一个字符串给...前端，描述发生了什么。换句话说，它允许我们将发生的错误转换为一个字符串，并将其流式传输到前端。在前端，你可以使用这个字符串来显示错误信息。我们的第一步是插入 `on error` 函数。我们将通过调用 `retria.is instance error` 来检查错误是否为 `retria` 错误。如果是，我们只需返回一条提示用户重试的消息。否则，如果重试不是 `retria` 错误，我们将返回一个默认消息。相信你之前也写过类似的代码，比如“发生未知错误”或类似内容。在前端，我们将获取 `use chat` 钩子中的 `error` 属性。这个属性可能是错误值，也可能是 `undefined`。在下方，我们不再传入 `to do`，而是渲染一个错误信息组件——就在当前文件底部的这个位置。完成这些后，你应该就能向系统发送消息了。无论发生什么情况，你都应该收到一个重试错误，此时错误信息组件中将显示你在 `on-error` 函数中返回的消息。 祝你好运，我们稍后在解决方案中见。

让我们问问它法国的首都是什么，结果它说“无法完成请求，请重试”。我很好奇，这段写得这么漂亮的错误文本是从哪儿来的呢？其实，它来自 API Chat 内部的这个 on error 回调。这里有“无法完成请求，请重试”的文本，也有“发生未知错误”的文本。在前端，我们从 useChat 中解构出 error 属性，一路向下，直到这里就是我们的错误消息。注意，error 对象总是包含一个 message 属性，因此我们就能知道在前端要渲染什么内容。在 UI 消息流本身设置这种错误处理机制非常方便，因为这意味着此处的任何错误都会冒泡到 create UI 消息流，并在这里被捕获。你可以把它看作是对 execute 函数内部所有操作的一个 try...catch。至于阅读部分，我建议你通读这些 ASTK 错误；如果你发现它们源自你的代码，现在你就知道该如何处理它们，并在前端呈现出美观的提示了。干得漂亮，我们下一节再见。

## P65 065 Building A Workflow

**原文**：

So far, we've mostly been dealing with agents as a setup. An agent is where you hand full control over the control flow of your application to the LLM. In other words, you give the LLM a bunch of tools and the LLM decides in which order to call the tools and how to respond to their outputs. This can be great for open-ended tasks where you might need to be creative to find the right path to the solution. but it's less good for tasks where you always know that one step must follow another step. So while agents hand over the control flow to the LLM, work flows are where the developer controls the control flow, and just uses the LLM to produce outputs. In the next few exercises we're going to look at work flows. How you can build them, but also how you can blend them with agent. You'll see what I mean as we go. In this first exercise, we're going to be creating a generator evaluator workflow, where we're first going to call an alarm to write a slack message, then tell an alarm to evaluate the slack message that was just created and then based on the feedback and the original slack message we're going to get it to create another slack message. We're going to do this quite simply for now, where we're going to use generate text to write the slack message, generate text again to write the evaluation, and then we're going to stream the final response. That will mean in our UI, we're waiting around a little bit for the stream to start, but we're going to fix that in the next few exercises. I've prepared for you a few system prompts here, one for creating the first draft, then for evaluating, then for creating the final one. And I've also given you a four- that message history function. You can use these to format the message history that we get from the body here. I found this just makes it a little bit easier to compose your prompts. Once that's done, you should be able to send a slight message complaining about a colleague or something and you'll wait a little bit until you see the final thing streaming back of you. Good luck and I'll see you in the solution.

**译文**：

到目前为止，我们主要将智能体作为一种设置来处理。智能体是指您将应用程序的控制流程完全交给大语言模型（LLM）来处理。换句话说，您为 LLM 提供一系列工具，由它决定调用这些工具的顺序以及如何响应工具的输出。这种方式非常适合需要创造性地寻找解决方案路径的开放式任务。但对于那些您始终知道某一步骤必须紧随另一步骤的任务来说，智能体的效果就不那么理想了。因此，当智能体将控制流程交给 LLM 时，工作流则是由开发者控制流程，仅使用 LLM 来生成输出。在接下来的几个练习中，我们将探讨工作流——如何构建它们，以及如何将它们与智能体相结合。随着学习的深入，您就会明白我的意思。在本练习中，我们将创建一个“生成-评估”工作流：首先调用一个智能体编写一条 Slack 消息，然后让另一个智能体评估刚刚创建的 Slack 消息，最后根据反馈和原始的 Slack 消息，再让它生成一条新的 Slack 消息。 我们现在会采用一个非常简单的方式：首先使用“生成文本”来编写 Slack 消息，再次使用“生成文本”来编写评估内容，然后流式传输最终响应。这意味着在用户界面中，我们需要稍等片刻，直到流式传输开始，不过我们会在接下来的几个练习中解决这个问题。我为你准备了一些系统提示，分别用于创建初稿、进行评估以及生成最终版本。此外，我还提供了一个四段式消息历史函数，你可以用它来处理从消息体中获取的消息历史。我发现这样做可以让提示词的编写变得稍微容易一些。完成这些之后，你应该就能发送一条抱怨同事之类的简短消息，然后稍等片刻，直到看到最终内容流式传输返回。祝你好运，我们稍后在解决方案中见。

---

好的，我们正在给老板写一条 Slack 消息，抱怨某位同事的卫生问题。我们会经历一段等待时间，然后内容开始流式输出。我们再运行一次看看。你可以看到首次生成 token 前的等待时间相当长，之后内容才开始流式输出。我们来看看这里的代码。我们调用 `generate_text` 方法，使用一个带有系统提示词（system prompt）的模型，并通过格式化消息历史（message history）传入对话历史。下一步，我们用另一次 `generate_text` 调用对 Slack 消息进行评估。这次我们传入对话历史，以及上一次结果中生成的 Slack 消息。最后，我们再调用一次，并将结果流式输出。我们流式传输文本，并返回 UI 消息响应（response），这当然会将其转换为流式输出，再将流式输出转换为最终响应。这里我们传入了初稿、之前的反馈以及完整的对话历史。这一过程就像一个工作流。最终我们得到了一个由三次 LLM 调用组成的确定性管道：我们先撰写，再评估，最后重写。尽管这个过程相对简单。 你的脑海中应该已经涌现出许多可以利用这些工作流实现的不同想法。接下来，我们要重点探讨如何优化这个非常基础的工作流，特别是提升“首次生成 token 的时间”，因为这些生成文本真是让我头疼。干得不错，我们下一节再见。

我们的工作流程目前运行得相当不错，也能生成一些像样的输出。但用户在屏幕上需要等待相当长的一段时间才能看到内容。这当然是因为我们使用的是“生成文本”（Generate Text）功能，而不是“流式文本”（Stream Text）。接下来，我们将改为使用流式文本，同时借此机会练习如何创建自定义数据部分。因为我想把初稿流式传输到前端，然后再单独流式传输评估内容，并希望这两者在前端界面上能够彼此区分。我在这里已经添加了一些必要的脚手架代码。我为你提供了一个带有“我的消息类型”（my message type）的“创建 UI 消息流”（create UI message stream）功能。然后我们还会将该流转换为“UI 消息流响应”（UI message stream response）。不过，这些只是脚手架部分。现在让我们具体看看你的待办事项示例。你的第一项任务是……明确自定义数据部分。我建议分别为评估内容和初稿各创建一个自定义数据部分。最终稿则可以像往常一样以普通文本部分的形式流式传输。此外，你还需要在这个小文件夹中将所有“UI 消息”（UI message）的实例替换为“我的消息”（my message）。 完成之后，就可以开始移除 `execute` 函数内部的 `generate text` 调用了。我会把这类东西留到最后处理。你需要做的是将每一个 `generate text` 调用改为 `stream text`，监控文本流，并在文本流产生的同时，将该数据部分流式传输到前端。你需要确保使用 ID 技巧，以便随时间推移更新同一个数据部分。下方有一些关于如何实现这一点的参考资料。一旦这两个部分都完成，你就可以处理下方的 `stream text` 调用。这个调用本身已经是流式传输的，这很好，但我们确实需要将其合并到写入器中。合并时，我们需要传入 `send start false`。默认情况下，当将一个 UI 消息流合并到另一个 UI 消息流中时，它会发送开始和结束部分。但因为我们已经在这里开始了消息，所以不希望它再次发送开始部分。实际上，我们希望手动开始。下方有一些关于开始和结束部分的参考资料。一切完成后，就可以转到前端了。我们将通过 `use chat my message` 将我们的 `my message type` 传递给这里的 `use chat` 钩子。 然后我们将转到消息组件。在这里，我们需要调整“部分”部分，使其成为“我的消息部分”，然后我们将渲染一些 UI，把这些部分呈现到前端。完成之后，您应该能看到工作流的每个部分都实时流式传输到前端。这将极大提升我们的首次令牌生成时间，用户也能清楚地了解工作流的每一个环节。干得漂亮，我们稍后在解决方案中再见。

Inside our code, we are manually writing the start part, so we're starting the message that we don't get any weird duplicate message. issues. We're then streaming in the right Slack result here by monitoring the text stream and then grabbing out a data Slack message type which we then write to the front end. We do the same thing further down with this evaluation Slack result with the text stream again now writing it as the feedback and then we get the final slackness. down here which again we just merge in and we don't send a start part because we've already sent one. This is working because we have a type up the top here of my message which has slack message where the data parts are string and the slack message feedback where the data value is a string. This is then being used in a bunch of different places in including in components where we use it to type them my message parts here. We then map over each part and render it to the front end. And because the parts are kept in the same order that they were streamed down, it means that we get a really nice view of everything that's happened so far in the stream. So hopefully this setup gives you a sense for how you can stream down. multiple parts of a workflow as they happen. Putting them in their own custom data part means you get a lot of flexibility in the front end with how you want to render them. 我们可以看到第一个草稿流式传输进来，接着反馈流式传输进来，然后我们得到最终文本。请注意，我们立即看到了第一个 token，并且可以在这里看到完整的交互历史记录。在我们的代码中，我们手动编写了起始部分，因此我们开始发送消息，这样就不会出现奇怪的消息重复问题。接着，我们通过监控文本流并提取出 Slack 消息类型的数据，将正确的 Slack 结果流式传输到这里，然后将其写入前端。在下方，我们对评估 Slack 结果也执行了相同的操作，再次通过文本流将其作为反馈写入，然后我们得到最终的 Slack 内容。这里我们同样将其合并进来，并且没有发送起始部分，因为我们之前已经发送过一次。这是因为我们在顶部定义了一个 `myMessage` 类型，其中包含 `slackMessage`（其数据部分为字符串）以及 `slackMessageFeedback`（其数据值为字符串）。该类型随后在多个地方被使用，包括在组件中，我们用它来为这里的 `myMessage` 部分添加类型。然后，我们对每个部分进行映射，并将其渲染到前端。 由于各个部分是按照它们被流式传输下来的顺序保留的，这意味着我们可以非常清晰地看到流式传输到目前为止发生的所有内容。因此，希望这种设置能让你了解如何在多个工作流部分发生时将它们流式传输下来。将它们放入自定义的数据部分中，意味着你在前端可以根据需要灵活地渲染这些部分。所以我发现，尽管这里有很多代码，你需要手动将文本流转换为自定义数据部分，但最终你会从中获得很多好处。干得不错，我们下一节再见。

## P69 069 Creating Your Own Loop

**原文**：

In our previous exercise, we went full workflow. In other words, we took complete control over the control flow of our application. It's written right there in the code that it must produce a first draft, then it must ask for feedback, then it must produce a next text output. In this workflow, we, as the developer, are driving the car and the AI is the passenger. But over the next two exercises... is I actually want to pass a bit of the control back to the AI and our first step here is going to be creating a loop. Instead of just taking this linear path where it does one thing and then another, we're going to run this evaluation loop a couple of times to see if we can get a better output. The way this loop is going to work is going to be a bit scary, we're going to use a while loop. We're going to do it in- side the execute function of the Create UI message stream. And to make our loop work, we're going to keep track of a few different variables. First of all, the current step that we're on, you can start this at one, we're on the first step, and then increment it each time. Then the most recent draft that's been produced by our system. Again, we're going to be creating multiple drafts here so we want to keep track of the most recent one. And then the most recent feedback that that draft has received. So the loop is going to work like this. It's going to write a slack message and save that in most recent draft. Then we'll evaluate the slack message and put that in most recent feedback. Then the loop will start again where it writes a draft based on the feedback and then we'll produce one more set of feedback and put that in most recent feedback. there's some reference material on how to do that if you need a code sample to copy. I have preserved for you down here all of the code that we had before. So a lot of this you're going to be wrapping in a while loop. So a quick reminder of the things you need to do in this while loop. First, create a draft, second, evaluate that draft, then increment the step count and then the while loop should be ready to run again. Your stop condition for the while loop is really really important. You probably only want to run this loop twice It would probably look something like this where we have wild step is less than or equal to two I think in the solution I start steps at zero, which means you want this instead where it be less than two I just want you to make double-eat triply sure that you have this stop condition set up before you start running your LLM because of course this is a paid service and we don't want to have to ping the paid service again and again again. Once that's done you should be able to see multiple drafts and multiple rounds of feedback. Good luck and I will see you in the solution.

**译文**：

在之前的练习中，我们走完了完整的工作流程。换句话说，我们完全掌控了应用程序的控制流。代码中明确写明：它必须先产出初稿，然后征求反馈，再产出下一版文本。在这个工作流程中，我们作为开发者是“驾驶员”，而 AI 是“乘客”。但在接下来的两个练习中……我实际上想把一部分控制权交还给 AI。我们的第一步就是创建一个循环。我们不再生硬地按部就班地执行“做一件事，再做另一件事”，而是让这个评估循环运行几次，看看是否能得到更优的输出。这个循环的实现方式会有点吓人——我们将使用一个 `while` 循环。我们会在 `Create UI message stream` 的 `execute` 函数内部实现它。为了让循环正常运行，我们需要跟踪几个不同的变量。首先是当前所处的步骤——你可以从 1 开始，也就是第一步，之后每执行一次就递增。其次是系统最近一次产出的草稿。 同样，我们将在此处创建多个草稿，因此需要跟踪最新的草稿，以及该草稿收到的最新反馈。循环的执行流程如下：首先编写一条 Slack 消息并将其保存到“最新草稿”中；接着评估这条 Slack 消息，并将评估结果存入“最新反馈”；然后循环重新开始，根据反馈编写新的草稿，再生成一组新的反馈并存入“最新反馈”。如果需要代码示例作为参考，这里有一些相关资料。我在下方为你保留了之前所有的代码。因此，你需要将大部分代码包裹在一个 while 循环中。快速回顾一下，在这个 while 循环中你需要完成以下步骤：第一，创建草稿；第二，评估该草稿；第三，将步长计数器加 1；之后 while 循环即可准备再次运行。while 循环的终止条件至关重要。 你可能只想让这个循环运行两次。它看起来大概会是这样的：当 `wild step` 小于或等于 2 时。我认为在解决方案中，我从 0 开始计算步数，这意味着你应该使用下面的写法，即让它小于 2。我只希望你 **double-eat triply** 确保在开始运行 LLM 之前设置好这个停止条件，因为这毕竟是一个付费服务，我们不想一次又一次地不断向付费服务发送请求。完成这一步后，你应该就能看到多个草稿和多轮反馈了。祝你好运，我们稍后在解决方案中见。

**原文**：

Okay, let's give this a go. We're going to write a slap message to our boss. We get the first draft. We get a load of feedback. We get another draft and then we get some feedback. And then we get the same draft. Could we chat briefly when you have a moment? Could we chat briefly when you have a moment? Yeah, we're streaming the most recent draft down as the final result. We can see two that are loop ghost. through two iterations. This first draft and feedback and then this draft and the feedback. The code looks like this. We are writing the start part manually. Then we start our step as zero and we initialize the most recent draft and the most recent feedback. Then we do our logic inside the while loop. We write our slack message here and then we stream it to the front. And then we take that initial draft that's been streamed and we save it in two most recent draft We then pass that most recent draft into the evaluation as well as any previous feedback to which in the first loop through will be empty But in later loops will have feedback in we then stream that feedback to the front end save it in the most recent feed back and then increment the step counter at the end. Finally, once that loop is finished, we get out here, we take the most recent draft and we stream it to the front end, using text art, text, delta, and text, and parts. So that's it, we have created a loop. And while this first loop that we've created is quite humble, quite simple, this idea of having full control of, the loop is incredibly powerful, and I'm going to show you why in the next exercise. **译文**：

好的，我们来试试。我们要给老板写一条Slack消息。我们拿到初稿。收到大量反馈。再拿到另一个草稿，然后收到一些反馈。然后我们又拿到同样的草稿。您有空的时候能简单聊几句吗？您有空的时候能简单聊几句吗？是的，我们正在把最新的草稿作为最终结果实时传输。我们可以看到两个循环迭代：第一次是初稿和反馈，然后是这个草稿和反馈。代码大致如下：我们手动编写起始部分。然后将步骤初始化为零，并初始化最新的草稿和最新的反馈。接着在while循环内部执行逻辑。我们在这里编写Slack消息，然后将其传输到前端。接着我们将已传输的初始草稿保存为“最新的草稿”。然后我们将这个最新的草稿以及之前的反馈（在第一次循环中为空，但在后续循环中会包含反馈）传入评估环节。随后，我们将评估反馈传输到前端，将其保存为“最新的反馈”，最后在末尾递增步骤计数器。 最后，当那个循环结束后，我们来到这里，取出最新的草稿，并通过 text art、text、delta 和 text parts 将其流式传输到前端。就这样，我们创建了一个循环。虽然我们创建的第一个循环相当朴素、简单，但完全掌控循环这一理念却极其强大，我将在下一个练习中向你展示其中的原因。或者，我很少能在这个结尾留下悬念。干得不错，我们下个练习再见。

## P71 071 Breaking the Loop Early

**原文**：

Let's take a long hard look at the result of our previous exercise. We have here a first draft which is really good. Even the LLN thing, so the message is professional and clear avoids being overly accusatory. So we've created a good draft and we've received feedback that it is good. Surely at that point we should break the loop. We don't then need to create another draft and then add more useful. this feedback. What I'm saying here is that the LLM should be in control of when the feedback is good enough. In other words, during the feedback step we need to do two things. We need to provide some feedback and we need to figure out if we're going to break out of the loop. Doing this will turn us again from being a more workflow driven setup to being a more agentic setup because the thing we know about... Now agents is that the LLM is in control of the control flow and figuring out when to stop, well that is a pretty important part of the control flow. The way we're going to do this is to take this stream text call that we're currently using just to generate feedback and instead we're going to get it to do two things at the same time. We wanted to say whether the draft is good enough and if it's not good enough, produce some feedback on how to improve it. This to me sounds like a structured object that we need to get back, where we need is good enough to be a boolean and feedback to be a string, which is optional only required if it's not good enough. So instead of stream text, we should be using stream object. Now this will necessitate a quite a few changes. particularly in how we're streaming the output to the front end. Instead of a text stream, this will be a partial object stream. Though of course, we'll only need to stream the feedback if it's not good enough. If it is good enough, then we'll simply break with a break keyword. And since the loop is broken, we'll then go to the bit after the loop, where we're just streaming out the most recent draft. to do this exercise really is around here, where we need to change this to a stream object call and pass it a schema, make sure to that you add descriptions to that schema so it understands. You may also need to go into the system prompt too, especially to look at its output format to explain what the output format is. Once that's done, we need to stream the output to the front end and once. that streaming is done and we have the final output. We can then choose to break the stream based on whether the feedback was good enough or not. Once this is done you should be able to query your system and it should only take as many feedback rounds as it needs. Though bear in mind our stop condition at the top will still stop it if it reaches two. Good luck and I'll see you in the solution.

**译文**：

让我们仔细审视一下上次练习的成果。我们这里已经有了一个非常不错的初稿。就连LLN部分也是如此，因此传递的信息既专业又清晰，同时避免了过度指责。所以我们已经创建了一份好的草稿，并且收到了“这份草稿不错”的反馈。那么，在这一点上，我们当然应该终止循环。此时就不需要再创建另一个草稿，然后继续添加更多“有用”的反馈了。我想说的是，LLM应该掌控何时认为反馈已经足够好。换句话说，在反馈阶段，我们需要完成两件事：一是提供反馈，二是判断是否要跳出循环。这样做会再次将我们从一种更偏向工作流驱动的架构，转变为一种更具自主性的架构，因为关于智能体（agents）这一点我们很清楚——LLM掌控着控制流程，而判断何时停止，正是控制流程中非常重要的一环。我们将通过以下方式实现这一点：不再仅使用当前用于生成反馈的流式文本调用（stream text call），而是让它同时完成两件事。 我们想判断草稿是否足够好，如果不够好，就提供一些改进建议。对我来说，这听起来像是一个结构化的对象，我们需要返回这个对象。其中“是否足够好”应该是一个布尔值，而“反馈”则是一个字符串，仅在草稿不够好时是必需的。因此，我们应该使用流对象（stream object）而不是流文本（stream text）。这将需要做出相当多的改动，尤其是在将输出流式传输到前端的方式上。我们将不再使用文本流，而是使用部分对象流（partial object stream）。当然，只有在草稿不够好的情况下，我们才需要传输反馈。如果草稿足够好，我们就直接通过 `break` 关键字中断循环。由于循环被中断，程序将跳转到循环之后的部分，此时我们只需传输最新的草稿即可。要完成这个任务，关键点在于将此处改为流对象调用，并传入一个模式（schema），同时确保为该模式添加描述信息，以便系统能够理解。你可能还需要修改系统提示（system prompt），特别是检查其输出格式，并明确说明输出格式的具体要求。 完成这一步后，我们需要将输出流式传输到前端。一旦流式传输完成并得到最终输出，我们就可以根据反馈是否足够好来决定是否中断流。完成这一步后，你就可以查询你的系统了，它只会进行所需的必要反馈轮次。不过请注意，我们顶部的停止条件仍然会在达到两轮时终止过程。祝你好运，我们稍后在解决方案中见。

## P72 072 Breaking the Loop Early Solution

**原文**：

In tree game, what's happening here? Well, the key is in our stream object call here, and particularly in the schema which is just down here. We have two pieces of the schema. We have a feedback property and an is good enough property. The feedback is a zod string here, which is optional. And I've specified only return this if the draft is not good enough. Then we have an is... good enough Boolean below saying whether the most recent draft is good enough to stop the loop. This means that because this first draft was considered good enough, the feedback was not streamed to the front end. In fact, wasn't even generated by the LLM. All the LLM generated was an object containing is good enough true. If we zoom down we can see that we are streaming the feedback to the front end. And if it exists, this means that if there's no feedback, then right to dot right will never be called. We grab the final object from evaluateslankresult.object and if the draft is good enough, break the loop. But if the draft is not good enough, then we take the feedback and we stick it in the most recent feedback variable. This sets up them with stream object deciding whether or not. break the loop. We're also handling a rare case here where it might not provide feedback. Our object here, this final evaluation object, has is good enough Boolean and feedback string or undefined. This means it could technically say is good enough false, but provide undefined as the feedback. You know, these are LLMs. They behave probabilistically. So in that rare case, we do go down and we say, if there's no feedback, we throw an error saying no feedback provided by the LLM. The set up then where the stream object call is deciding the control flow of our program is I think a really, really nice way to think about mixing a gintick and workflow setups. Because inside this loop we can run as well. much deterministic logic as we want. We can break the loop deterministically any time. Or we can hand more control over to the LLM via stream object calls where we say, if this boolean is true, then break the loop. I've built lots and lots of fun things using this setup. You can even get the LLM to choose between multiple different options via z.inum, and all while still... streaming to the front end the entire time. Hopefully that gets your brain worrying with different ideas of things you could build with this. Nice work and I will see you in the next one.

**译文**：

在树形游戏中，这里发生了什么？关键在于我们这里的流对象调用，尤其是位于下方的模式（schema）。该模式包含两个部分：一个是 `feedback` 属性，另一个是 `is good enough` 属性。其中 `feedback` 是一个 Zod 字符串，属于可选字段。我特别规定，仅当草稿（draft）不够好时才返回该字段。接下来是一个布尔值 `is... good enough`，用于表示最新的草稿是否足以停止循环。这意味着由于第一个草稿被认为已经足够好，因此 `feedback` 并未被流式传输到前端，甚至根本没有被 LLM 生成。LLM 唯一生成的内容是一个包含 `is good enough: true` 的对象。如果我们向下查看，可以看到我们正在将 `feedback` 流式传输到前端。如果 `feedback` 存在，这意味着如果没有 `feedback`，则 `rightTo.right` 永远不会被调用。我们从 `evaluateslankresult.object` 中获取最终对象，如果草稿足够好，就跳出循环；但如果草稿不够好，则将 `feedback` 存入最新的 `feedback` 变量中。这样就为流对象提供了判断是否跳出循环的依据。 我们在这里还处理一种罕见情况：模型可能不提供反馈。我们这里的对象——这个最终评估对象——包含一个 `isGoodEnough` 布尔值和一个反馈字符串（可能为 `undefined`）。这意味着从技术上讲，它可能返回 `isGoodEnough: false`，但反馈却为 `undefined`。毕竟，这些是 LLM（大型语言模型），它们的行为具有概率性。因此，在这种罕见情况下，我们会执行以下逻辑：如果没有提供反馈，就抛出错误，提示“LLM 未提供反馈”。 我认为，这种通过流对象调用来决定程序控制流程的架构，是将 `gintick` 和工作流设置相结合的一种非常巧妙的方式。因为在这个循环内部，我们可以运行任意多的确定性逻辑，也可以随时以确定性方式中断循环。或者，我们也可以通过流对象调用将更多控制权交给 LLM，例如：如果该布尔值为 `true`，则中断循环。 我利用这种架构构建了许多有趣的应用。你甚至可以通过 `z.inum` 让 LLM 在多个不同选项之间进行选择，同时在整个过程中持续向前端流式传输内容。 希望这能激发你的灵感，让你开始思考可以用它来构建各种各样的东西。干得不错，我们下一期再见。
